CURA first fine-tunes domain-specific clinical LMs to obtain task-adapted patient embeddings, and then performs uncertainty fine-tuning of a multi-head classifier using a bi-level uncertainty objective. Specifically, an individual-level calibration term aligns predictive uncertainty with each patient's likelihood of error, while a cohort-aware regularizer pulls risk estimates toward event rates in their local neighborhoods in the embedding space and places extra weight on ambiguous cohorts near the decision boundary. We further show that this cohort-aware term can be interpreted as a cross-entropy loss with neighborhood-informed soft labels, providing a label-smoothing view of our method. Extensive experiments on MIMIC-IV clinical risk prediction tasks across various clinical LMs show that CURA consistently improves calibration metrics without substantially compromising discrimination. Further analysis illustrates that CURA reduces overconfident false reassurance and yields more trustworthy uncertainty estimates for downstream clinical decision support.

</details>


### [40] [SPAGBias: Uncovering and Tracing Structured Spatial Gender Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14672)
*Binxian Su,Haoye Lou,Shucheng Zhu,Weikang Wang,Ying Liu,Dong Yu,Pengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPAGBias框架首次系统评估LLM中的空间性别偏见，发现模型在62种城市微空间中形成结构化性别-空间关联，这些偏见在模型训练流程中被嵌入和强化，并在实际应用中产生具体失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在城市规划中应用增加，但性别空间理论指出性别等级制度已嵌入空间组织，因此担忧LLM可能再现或放大此类偏见。需要系统评估LLM中的空间性别偏见。

Method: 提出SPAGBias框架：包含62种城市微空间的分类法、提示库和三个诊断层（显性-强制选择重采样、概率性-词元级不对称、建构性-语义和叙事角色分析）。测试六个代表性模型，分析提示设计、温度和模型规模对偏见表达的影响，追踪训练流程中的偏见形成机制。

Result: 识别出超越公私领域划分的结构化性别-空间关联，形成细微的微观层面映射。故事生成显示情感、措辞和社会角色共同塑造"空间性别叙事"。模型关联显著超过现实世界分布，追踪实验表明偏见在预训练、指令微调和奖励建模流程中被嵌入和强化。下游实验显示这些偏见在规范性和描述性应用场景中产生具体失败。

Conclusion: 该研究将社会学理论与计算分析相结合，将偏见研究扩展到空间领域，揭示了LLM如何通过语言编码社会性别认知，为理解模型偏见提供了新的理论框架和方法论工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being increasingly used in urban planning, but since gendered space theory highlights how gender hierarchies are embedded in spatial organization, there is concern that LLMs may reproduce or amplify such biases. We introduce SPAGBias - the first systematic framework to evaluate spatial gender bias in LLMs. It combines a taxonomy of 62 urban micro-spaces, a prompt library, and three diagnostic layers: explicit (forced-choice resampling), probabilistic (token-level asymmetry), and constructional (semantic and narrative role analysis). Testing six representative models, we identify structured gender-space associations that go beyond the public-private divide, forming nuanced micro-level mappings. Story generation reveals how emotion, wording, and social roles jointly shape "spatial gender narratives". We also examine how prompt design, temperature, and model scale influence bias expression. Tracing experiments indicate that these patterns are embedded and reinforced across the model pipeline (pre-training, instruction tuning, and reward modeling), with model associations found to substantially exceed real-world distributions. Downstream experiments further reveal that such biases produce concrete failures in both normative and descriptive application settings. This work connects sociological theory with computational analysis, extending bias research into the spatial domain and uncovering how LLMs encode social gender cognition through language.

</details>


### [41] [Which bird does not have wings: Negative-constrained KGQA with Schema-guided Semantic Matching and Self-directed Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14749)
*Midan Shim,Seokju Hwang,Kaehyun Um,Kyong-Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对知识图谱问答中负约束被忽视的问题，提出了NEST-KGQA新任务和数据集，设计了PyLF逻辑形式，并开发了CUCKOO框架来处理多约束问题，在少样本设置下优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型具有强大的推理能力，但在忠实度和幻觉方面仍存在问题。在知识图谱问答中，现有基准和方法偏向于正约束和计算约束，而负约束在现实问题中频繁出现却被忽视，这限制了KGQA系统的实际应用能力。

Method: 1) 提出NEgative-conSTrained (NEST) KGQA新任务，每个问题至少包含一个负约束；2) 创建NestKGQA数据集；3) 设计Python格式的逻辑形式PyLF，以清晰表达否定同时保持可读性；4) 开发CUCKOO框架，首先生成约束感知的逻辑形式草稿，进行模式引导的语义匹配，然后仅在执行不当逻辑形式产生空结果时选择性应用自导向精炼，以降低成本同时提高鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果表明，CUCKOO在少样本设置下，在传统KGQA基准和NEST-KGQA基准上都一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了KGQA中负约束处理的空白，提出的NEST-KGQA任务、PyLF逻辑形式和CUCKOO框架有效解决了多约束问题的语义复杂性，提高了KGQA系统处理现实世界问题的能力。

Abstract: Large language models still struggle with faithfulness and hallucinations despite their remarkable reasoning abilities. In Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA), semantic parsing-based approaches address the limitations by understanding constraints in a user's question and converting them into a logical form to execute on a knowledge graph. However, existing KGQA benchmarks and methods are biased toward positive and calculation constraints. Negative constraints are neglected, although they frequently appear in real-world questions. In this paper, we introduce a new task, NEgative-conSTrained (NEST) KGQA, where each question contains at least one negative constraint, and a corresponding dataset, NestKGQA. We also design PyLF, a Python-formatted logical form, since existing logical forms are hardly suitable to express negation clearly while maintaining readability. Furthermore, NEST questions naturally contain multiple constraints. To mitigate their semantic complexity, we present a novel framework named CUCKOO, specialized to multiple-constrained questions and ensuring semantic executability. CUCKOO first generates a constraint-aware logical form draft and performs schema-guided semantic matching. It then selectively applies self-directed refinement only when executing improper logical forms yields an empty result, reducing cost while improving robustness. Experimental results demonstrate that CUCKOO consistently outperforms baselines on both conventional KGQA and NEST-KGQA benchmarks under few-shot settings.

</details>


### [42] [CoPA: Benchmarking Personalized Question Answering with Data-Informed Cognitive Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14773)
*Hang Su,Zequn Liu,Chen Hu,Xuesong Lu,Yingce Xia,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoPA：基于社区-个体偏好分歧挖掘的个性化QA评估基准，包含1,985个用户配置文件，通过六个个性化因素进行细粒度评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在问答任务中展现潜力，但个性化评估存在瓶颈。现有方法主要依赖词法相似度或人工启发式规则，缺乏数据驱动的验证。需要更全面的个性化QA评估标准。

Method: 通过挖掘社区-个体偏好分歧（CIPD）——即个体选择超越共识的情况，提炼出六个关键个性化因素作为评估维度。基于此构建CoPA基准，包含1,985个用户配置文件，通过量化模型输出与用户特定认知偏好的对齐程度进行评估。

Result: 开发了CoPA基准，提供比通用指标更全面、更具区分度的个性化QA评估标准。代码已开源。

Conclusion: CoPA通过数据驱动的个性化因素评估，解决了现有个性化QA评估方法的局限性，为LLM的个性化能力提供了更精细的评估框架。

Abstract: While LLMs have demonstrated remarkable potential in Question Answering (QA), evaluating personalization remains a critical bottleneck. Existing paradigms predominantly rely on lexical-level similarity or manual heuristics, often lacking sufficient data-driven validation. We address this by mining Community-Individual Preference Divergence (CIPD), where individual choices override consensus, to distill six key personalization factors as evaluative dimensions. Accordingly, we introduce CoPA, a benchmark with 1,985 user profiles for fine-grained, factor-level assessment. By quantifying the alignment between model outputs and user-specific cognitive preferences inferred from interaction patterns, CoPA provides a more comprehensive and discriminative standard for evaluating personalized QA than generic metrics. The code is available at https://github.com/bjzgcai/CoPA.

</details>


### [43] [Knowing When Not to Answer: Evaluating Abstention in Multimodal Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14799)
*Nishanth Madhusudhan,Vikas Yadav,Alexandre Lacoste*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MM-AQA是一个新的多模态基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型和多智能体系统在证据不足时的有效弃权能力，发现现有模型很少主动弃权，需要弃权感知训练而非更好的提示或更多智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估范式假设所有问题都可回答，迫使模型总是回应，而多模态环境中的弃权能力研究不足。当前基准要么忽略不可回答性，要么依赖粗糙方法，无法捕捉真实失败模式。

Method: 引入MM-AQA基准，通过视觉模态依赖性和证据充分性两个维度，从可回答实例构建不可回答实例。评估了三个前沿视觉语言模型（闭源和开源）和两种多智能体架构，共2079个样本。

Result: 1) 标准提示下，VLMs很少弃权；简单置信度基线优于此设置。2) MAS改善弃权但引入准确率-弃权权衡。3) 顺序设计匹配或优于迭代变体，表明瓶颈是校准错误而非推理深度。4) 模型在图像或文本证据缺失时弃权，但在证据退化或矛盾时尝试调和。

Conclusion: 有效的多模态弃权需要弃权感知训练，而非更好的提示或更多智能体。MM-AQA基准为系统评估多模态环境中的弃权能力提供了新框架。

Abstract: Effective abstention (EA), recognizing evidence insufficiency and refraining from answering, is critical for reliable multimodal systems. Yet existing evaluation paradigms for vision-language models (VLMs) and multi-agent systems (MAS) assume answerability, pushing models to always respond. Abstention has been studied in text-only settings but remains underexplored multimodally; current benchmarks either ignore unanswerability or rely on coarse methods that miss realistic failure modes. We introduce MM-AQA, a benchmark that constructs unanswerable instances from answerable ones via transformations along two axes: visual modality dependency and evidence sufficiency. Evaluating three frontier VLMs spanning closed and open-source models and two MAS architectures across 2079 samples, we find: (1) under standard prompting, VLMs rarely abstain; even simple confidence baselines outperform this setup, (2) MAS improves abstention but introduces an accuracy-abstention trade-off, (3) sequential designs match or exceed iterative variants, suggesting the bottleneck is miscalibration rather than reasoning depth, and (4) models abstain when image or text evidence is absent, but attempt reconciliation with degraded or contradictory evidence. Effective multimodal abstention requires abstention-aware training rather than better prompting or more agents.

</details>


### [44] [Modeling LLM Unlearning as an Asymmetric Two-Task Learning Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14808)
*Zeguan Xiao,Siqing Li,Yong Wang,Xuetao Wei,Jian Yang,Yun Chen,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出保留优先的梯度合成框架SAGO，将LLM遗忘问题重构为不对称双任务问题，通过解耦任务特定梯度提取和冲突感知组合来缓解遗忘-保留权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型遗忘旨在移除特定知识同时保持通用能力，现有方法在遗忘与保留之间存在权衡，需要更好的解决方案来平衡这两个目标

Method: 将LLM遗忘重构为不对称双任务问题（保留为主目标，遗忘为辅助），提出保留优先的梯度合成框架，包含任务特定梯度提取和冲突感知组合两个阶段，具体实现包括PCGrad变体和SAGO方法

Result: 在WMDP Bio/Cyber和RWKU基准测试中，SAGO持续推动帕累托前沿：在WMDP Bio（SimNPO+GD）上，目标模型MMLU性能恢复从44.6%（朴素方法）提升到94.0%（+PCGrad）再到96.0%（+SAGO），同时保持相当的遗忘强度

Conclusion: 重塑梯度几何而非重新平衡损失是缓解遗忘-保留权衡的关键，保留优先的梯度合成框架通过解耦任务特定梯度提取和冲突感知组合有效解决了LLM遗忘问题

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to remove targeted knowledge while preserving general capability. In this paper, we recast LLM unlearning as an asymmetric two-task problem: retention is the primary objective and forgetting is an auxiliary. From this perspective, we propose a retention-prioritized gradient synthesis framework that decouples task-specific gradient extraction from conflict-aware combination. Instantiating the framework, we adapt established PCGrad to resolve gradient conflicts, and introduce SAGO, a novel retention-prioritized gradient synthesis method. Theoretically, both variants ensure non-negative cosine similarity with the retain gradient, while SAGO achieves strictly tighter alignment through constructive sign-constrained synthesis. Empirically, on WMDP Bio/Cyber and RWKU benchmarks, SAGO consistently pushes the Pareto frontier: e.g., on WMDP Bio (SimNPO+GD), recovery of target model MMLU performance progresses from 44.6% (naive) to 94.0% (+PCGrad) and further to 96.0% (+SAGO), while maintaining comparable forgetting strength. Our results show that re-shaping gradient geometry, rather than re-balancing losses, is the key to mitigating unlearning-retention trade-offs.

</details>

*Rami Luisto,Liisa Petäinen,Tommi Grönholm,Jan Böhm,Maarit Ahtiainen,Tomi Lilja,Ilkka Pölönen,Sami Äyrämö*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在芬兰语医疗文本上对芬兰BERT模型进行领域微调的效果，并尝试通过观察嵌入空间几何变化来预测领域特定预训练的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗AI领域，获取标注数据通常需要很长时间，特别是在标签方面存在延迟。当标注数据稀缺时，对无标注数据进行领域微调是NLP分类任务的常用方法。

Method: 1. 在芬兰语医疗文本数据上对芬兰BERT模型进行领域微调；2. 通过观察领域微调导致的嵌入空间几何变化，尝试预测领域特定预训练的收益。

Result: 论文报告了芬兰BERT在芬兰医疗文本上领域微调的观察结果，以及通过嵌入几何变化预测领域预训练收益的尝试。

Conclusion: 研究探索了在医疗AI数据获取延迟背景下，通过领域微调和嵌入几何分析来优化模型性能的方法。

Abstract: In NLP classification tasks where little labeled data exists, domain fine-tuning of transformer models on unlabeled data is an established approach. In this paper we have two aims.
  (1) We describe our observations from fine-tuning the Finnish BERT model on Finnish medical text data.
  (2) We report on our attempts to predict the benefit of domain-specific pre-training of Finnish BERT from observing the geometry of embedding changes due to domain fine-tuning.
  Our driving motivation is the common\situation in healthcare AI where we might experience long delays in acquiring datasets, especially with respect to labels.

</details>


### [46] [Exploring and Testing Skill-Based Behavioral Profile Annotation: Human Operability and LLM Feasibility under Schema-Guided Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14843)
*Yufeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将行为特征标注视为多种标注技能的集合而非单一任务，通过技能文件驱动的流程评估LLM辅助标注，发现GPT-5.4在保留技能上表现可靠但具有选择性，开源模型存在模式转换问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为特征标注难以自动化，因为它需要同时处理多个语言学维度。研究旨在从技能角度而非任务层面评估LLM辅助标注的可行性，探索标注技能的可操作性分类。

Method: 使用3,134个中文隐喻色彩词衍生物的共现行和14特征行为特征模式，采用技能文件驱动的标注流程（通过模式文件、决策规则和示例外部定义每个特征）。两位人工标注者在300实例验证子集上完成两轮仅模式协议，将行为特征技能分为可直接操作、可恢复和结构未指定三类。然后评估GPT-5.4和三个本地可部署开源模型。

Result: 行为特征标注在技能层面高度异质：5个技能可直接操作，4个可通过聚焦重标注恢复，5个保持结构未指定。GPT-5.4执行保留技能具有实质性可靠性（准确率=0.678，κ=0.665，加权F1=0.695），但可行性是选择性的而非全局的。人类和GPT困难模式在技能层面高度一致（r=0.881），但在实例层面（r=0.016）和词汇项层面（r=-0.142）不一致，呈现"共享分类、独立执行"模式。成对一致性表明GPT应被视为独立的第三技能声音而非人类直接替代品。开源模型失败集中在模式到技能执行问题。

Conclusion: 自动标注应在技能可行性而非任务层面自动化方面进行评估。行为特征标注的可行性是技能特定的，GPT表现出与人类相似的技能层面困难模式但执行独立，表明LLM辅助标注应被视为独立标注声音而非人类替代品。

Abstract: Behavioral Profile (BP) annotation is difficult to automate because it requires simultaneous coding across multiple linguistic dimensions. We treat BP annotation as a bundle of annotation skills rather than a single task and evaluate LLM-assisted BP annotation from this perspective. Using 3,134 concordance lines of 30 Chinese metaphorical color-term derivatives and a 14-feature BP schema, we implement a skill-file-driven pipeline in which each feature is externally defined through schema files, decision rules, and examples. Two human annotators completed a two-round schema-only protocol on a 300-instance validation subset, enabling BP skills to be classified as directly operable, recoverable under focused re-annotation, or structurally underspecified. GPT-5.4 and three locally deployable open-source models were then evaluated under the same setup. Results show that BP annotation is highly heterogeneous at the skill level: 5 skills are directly operable, 4 are recoverable after focused re-annotation, and 5 remain structurally underspecified. GPT-5.4 executes the retained skills with substantial reliability (accuracy = 0.678, \k{appa} = 0.665, weighted F1 = 0.695), but this feasibility is selective rather than global. Human and GPT difficulty profiles are strongly aligned at the skill level (r = 0.881), but not at the instance level (r = 0.016) or lexical-item level (r = -0.142), a pattern we describe as shared taxonomy, independent execution. Pairwise agreement further suggests that GPT is better understood as an independent third skill voice than as a direct human substitute. Open-source failures are concentrated in schema-to-skill execution problems. These findings suggest that automatic annotation should be evaluated in terms of skill feasibility rather than task-level automation.

</details>


### [47] [Segment-Level Coherence for Robust Harmful Intent Probing in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14865)
*Xuanli He,Bilgehan Sel,Faizan Ali,Jenny Bao,Hoagy Cunningham,Jerry Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的流式探测方法，通过要求多个证据令牌一致支持预测而非依赖孤立的高分令牌，显著提升了CBRN领域有害内容检测的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在CBRN等高风险领域面临自适应越狱攻击，现有流式探测方法依赖少数高分令牌，在良性上下文中出现敏感CBRN术语时会产生误报。

Method: 引入流式探测目标，要求多个证据令牌一致支持预测而非依赖孤立峰值，基于聚合信号而非单令牌线索进行更鲁棒的检测，并比较了注意力、MLP激活和残差流特征的性能。

Result: 在1%误报率下，相比强流式基线，真阳性率相对提升35.55%；AUROC显著提升，即使基线性能已接近饱和（AUROC=97.40%）；注意力或MLP激活探测始终优于残差流特征；即使对抗微调启用新型字符级密码，有害意图仍可检测，基础LLM开发的探测器可即插即用，AUROC超过98.85%。

Conclusion: 多证据令牌聚合的流式探测方法能有效提升CBRN领域有害内容检测的鲁棒性和准确性，即使面对新型对抗攻击也能保持高性能，为高风险领域LLM安全监控提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly exposed to adaptive jailbreaking, particularly in high-stakes Chemical, Biological, Radiological, and Nuclear (CBRN) domains. Although streaming probes enable real-time monitoring, they still make systematic errors. We identify a core issue: existing methods often rely on a few high-scoring tokens, leading to false alarms when sensitive CBRN terms appear in benign contexts. To address this, we introduce a streaming probing objective that requires multiple evidence tokens to consistently support a prediction, rather than relying on isolated spikes. This encourages more robust detection based on aggregated signals instead of single-token cues. At a fixed 1% false-positive rate, our method improves the true-positive rate by 35.55% relative to strong streaming baselines. We further observe substantial gains in AUROC, even when starting from near-saturated baseline performance (AUROC = 97.40%). We also show that probing Attention or MLP activations consistently outperforms residual-stream features. Finally, even when adversarial fine-tuning enables novel character-level ciphers, harmful intent remains detectable: probes developed for the base LLMs can be applied ``plug-and-play'' to these obfuscated attacks, achieving an AUROC of over 98.85%.

</details>


### [48] [Reasoning Dynamics and the Limits of Monitoring Modality Reliance in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14888)
*Danae Sánchez Villegas,Samuel Lewis-Lim,Nikolaos Aletras,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了18个视觉语言模型(VLMs)的推理动态，发现模型存在"答案惯性"问题，早期预测在推理过程中被强化而非修正。虽然推理训练模型显示出更强的纠正行为，但收益受模态条件影响。模型易受误导性文本线索影响，即使视觉证据充分，且思维链(CoT)仅能部分揭示模态依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型(VLMs)展现出推理能力，但其如何整合视觉和文本信息的过程仍不清晰。研究者旨在分析VLMs的推理动态，特别是思维链(CoT)如何揭示模型决策过程中不同模态的贡献，这对于理解多模态系统的透明度和安全性至关重要。

Method: 研究分析了18个VLMs，涵盖指令调优和推理训练模型，来自两个不同的模型家族。方法包括：跟踪思维链过程中的置信度变化、测量推理的纠正效果、评估中间推理步骤的贡献。通过使用误导性文本线索进行受控干预，测试模型在不同模态条件下的表现。

Result: 1. 模型存在"答案惯性"：早期预测在推理过程中被强化而非修正。2. 推理训练模型显示出更强的纠正行为，但收益受模态条件影响(从文本主导到纯视觉设置)。3. 模型易受误导性文本线索影响，即使视觉证据充分。4. 思维链仅能部分揭示模态依赖：推理训练模型更可能明确提及线索，但其流畅的CoT可能掩盖实际对文本的依赖；指令调优模型较少明确提及线索，但其简短的痕迹能揭示与视觉输入的不一致。

Conclusion: 思维链仅提供了VLMs决策过程中不同模态驱动作用的有限视角。模型存在系统性偏差，如答案惯性和对误导性文本线索的敏感性。这些发现对多模态系统的透明度和安全性有重要影响，表明需要更全面的方法来理解和评估VLMs的推理过程。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision language models (VLMs) offer reasoning capabilities, yet how these unfold and integrate visual and textual information remains unclear. We analyze reasoning dynamics in 18 VLMs covering instruction-tuned and reasoning-trained models from two different model families. We track confidence over Chain-of-Thought (CoT), measure the corrective effect of reasoning, and evaluate the contribution of intermediate reasoning steps. We find that models are prone to answer inertia, in which early commitments to a prediction are reinforced, rather than revised during reasoning steps. While reasoning-trained models show stronger corrective behavior, their gains depend on modality conditions, from text-dominant to vision-only settings. Using controlled interventions with misleading textual cues, we show that models are consistently influenced by these cues even when visual evidence is sufficient, and assess whether this influence is recoverable from CoT. Although this influence can appear in the CoT, its detectability varies across models and depends on what is being monitored. Reasoning-trained models are more likely to explicitly refer to the cues, but their longer and fluent CoTs can still appear visually grounded while actually following textual cues, obscuring modality reliance. In contrast, instruction-tuned models refer to the cues less explicitly, but their shorter traces reveal inconsistencies with the visual input. Taken together, these findings indicate that CoT provides only a partial view of how different modalities drive VLM decisions, with important implications for the transparency and safety of multimodal systems.

</details>


### [49] [Comparison of Modern Multilingual Text Embedding Techniques for Hate Speech Detection Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14907)
*Evaldas Vaiciukynas,Paulius Danenas,Linas Ablonskis,Algirdas Sukys,Edgaras Dambrauskas,Voldemaras Zitkus,Rita Butkiene,Rimantas Butleris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估多语言句子嵌入模型在立陶宛语、俄语和英语仇恨言论检测中的表现，引入LtHate新语料库，比较六种嵌入模型，发现二分类监督模型显著优于单类异常检测，PCA压缩在监督学习中保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线仇恨言论和辱骂性语言在多语言环境及低资源语言（如立陶宛语）中构成日益严峻的挑战，需要研究现代多语言句子嵌入模型在这些语言中的仇恨言论检测能力。

Method: 引入LtHate立陶宛语仇恨言论语料库，使用统一Python流水线在LtHate、RuToxic和EnSuperset数据集上评估六种多语言编码器（potion、gemma、bge、snow、jina、e5）。对每种嵌入训练单类HBOS异常检测器和二类CatBoost分类器，并测试有无PCA压缩到64维特征向量的效果。

Result: 在所有数据集中，二分类监督模型始终显著优于单类异常检测。最佳配置在立陶宛语（jina）达到80.96%准确率和0.887 AUC ROC，俄语（e5）达到92.19%准确率和0.978 AUC ROC，英语（e5+PCA）达到77.21%准确率和0.859 AUC ROC。PCA压缩在监督设置中几乎保留了所有判别能力，但对无监督异常检测有负面影响。

Conclusion: 现代多语言句子嵌入结合梯度提升决策树为多语言仇恨言论检测应用提供了稳健的软计算解决方案，PCA压缩在监督学习中能有效减少特征维度而不显著损失性能。

Abstract: Online hate speech and abusive language pose a growing challenge for content moderation, especially in multilingual settings and for low-resource languages such as Lithuanian. This paper investigates to what extent modern multilingual sentence embedding models can support accurate hate speech detection in Lithuanian, Russian, and English, and how their performance depends on downstream modeling choices and feature dimensionality. We introduce LtHate, a new Lithuanian hate speech corpus derived from news portals and social networks, and benchmark six modern multilingual encoders (potion, gemma, bge, snow, jina, e5) on LtHate, RuToxic, and EnSuperset using a unified Python pipeline. For each embedding, we train both a one class HBOS anomaly detector and a two class CatBoost classifier, with and without principal component analysis (PCA) compression to 64-dimensional feature vectors. Across all datasets, two class supervised models consistently and substantially outperform one class anomaly detection, with the best configurations achieving up to 80.96% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.887 in Lithuanian (jina), 92.19% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.978 in Russian (e5), and 77.21% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.859 in English (e5 with PCA). PCA compression preserves almost all discriminative power in the supervised setting, while showing some negative impact for the unsupervised anomaly detection case. These results demonstrate how modern multilingual sentence embeddings combined with gradient boosted decision trees provide robust soft-computing solutions for multilingual hate speech detection applications.

</details>


### [50] [IE as Cache: Information Extraction Enhanced Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14930)
*Hang Lv,Sheng Liang,Hongchao Gu,Wei Guo,Defu Lian,Yong Liu,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出IE-as-Cache框架，将信息抽取重新定位为认知缓存而非终端目标，通过查询驱动抽取和缓存感知推理提升智能体推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息抽取仅作为终端目标，抽取结果孤立使用而非在多步推理中维护重用，限制了其在复杂推理任务中的潜力

Method: 提出IE-as-Cache框架，借鉴计算机分层内存思想，结合查询驱动抽取和缓存感知推理，动态维护紧凑中间信息并过滤噪声

Result: 在多样化LLM的挑战性基准测试中，推理准确性显著提升，证明信息抽取可作为可重用认知资源有效增强推理能力

Conclusion: 信息抽取可重新定位为认知缓存而非终端目标，为信息抽取的下游应用提供了有前景的研究方向

Abstract: Information Extraction aims to distill structured, decision-relevant information from unstructured text, serving as a foundation for downstream understanding and reasoning. However, it is traditionally treated merely as a terminal objective: once extracted, the resulting structure is often consumed in isolation rather than maintained and reused during multi-step inference. Moving beyond this, we propose \textit{IE-as-Cache}, a framework that repurposes IE as a cognitive cache to enhance agentic reasoning. Drawing inspiration from hierarchical computer memory, our approach combines query-driven extraction with cache-aware reasoning to dynamically maintain compact intermediate information and filter noise. Experiments on challenging benchmarks across diverse LLMs demonstrate significant improvements in reasoning accuracy, indicating that IE can be effectively repurposed as a reusable cognitive resource and offering a promising direction for future research on downstream uses of IE.

</details>


### [51] [XQ-MEval: A Dataset with Cross-lingual Parallel Quality for Benchmarking Translation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14934)
*Jingxuan Liu,Zhi Qu,Jin Tei,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Lemao Liu,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: XQ-MEval：一个半自动构建的数据集，用于评估多语言翻译指标，揭示跨语言评分偏差问题，并提出归一化策略改善评估公平性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言翻译系统评估通常采用跨语言平均指标分数的方法，但这种方法存在可疑之处，因为指标可能存在跨语言评分偏差——同等质量的翻译在不同语言中获得不同分数。由于缺乏提供跨语言平行质量实例的基准数据集，且专家标注不现实，这一问题尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 提出XQ-MEval数据集构建方法：1）将MQM定义的错误自动注入黄金翻译中；2）通过母语者过滤以确保可靠性；3）合并错误生成具有可控质量的伪翻译；4）将伪翻译与对应源文本和参考翻译组成三元组，用于评估翻译指标质量。覆盖9个翻译方向。

Result: 使用XQ-MEval对9个代表性指标进行实验，揭示了平均分数与人类判断之间的不一致性，首次提供了跨语言评分偏差的经验证据。发现指标在不同语言间存在系统性评分差异。

Conclusion: 基于XQ-MEval提出归一化策略，通过调整不同语言的分数分布，改善多语言指标评估的公平性和可靠性。XQ-MEval为系统研究跨语言评分偏差提供了首个基准数据集。

Abstract: Automatic evaluation metrics are essential for building multilingual translation systems. The common practice of evaluating these systems is averaging metric scores across languages, yet this is suspicious since metrics may suffer from cross-lingual scoring bias, where translations of equal quality receive different scores across languages. This problem has not been systematically studied because no benchmark exists that provides parallel-quality instances across languages, and expert annotation is not realistic. In this work, we propose XQ-MEval, a semi-automatically built dataset covering nine translation directions, to benchmark translation metrics. Specifically, we inject MQM-defined errors into gold translations automatically, filter them by native speakers for reliability, and merge errors to generate pseudo translations with controllable quality. These pseudo translations are then paired with corresponding sources and references to form triplets used in assessing the qualities of translation metrics. Using XQ-MEval, our experiments on nine representative metrics reveal the inconsistency between averaging and human judgment and provide the first empirical evidence of cross-lingual scoring bias. Finally, we propose a normalization strategy derived from XQ-MEval that aligns score distributions across languages, improving the fairness and reliability of multilingual metric evaluation.

</details>


### [52] [Text2Arch: A Dataset for Generating Scientific Architecture Diagrams from Natural Language Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14941)
*Shivank Garg,Sankalp Mittal,Manish Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一个自动从文本生成科学架构图的系统，通过创建新数据集和微调语言模型，在语义保真度上超越现有基线，性能与GPT-4o相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯文本描述复杂系统设计或科学过程效率低下且易产生歧义，需要自动从文本生成高语义保真度科学架构图的系统，应用于企业架构可视化、AI驱动软件设计和教育内容创建等领域。

Method: 1) 创建包含科学架构图像、对应文本描述和DOT代码表示的全面数据集；2) 利用该数据集微调小型语言模型；3) 使用GPT-4o进行上下文学习；4) 通过语言模型对输入文本进行语义理解，生成可处理为架构图的中间代码。

Result: 提出的模型显著优于DiagramAgent等现有基线模型，性能与基于GPT-4o上下文学习的生成结果相当，代码、数据和模型已公开。

Conclusion: 通过创建高质量数据集和微调语言模型，成功实现了从文本自动生成高保真科学架构图的任务，填补了该领域缺乏有效开源模型的空白。

Abstract: Communicating complex system designs or scientific processes through text alone is inefficient and prone to ambiguity. A system that automatically generates scientific architecture diagrams from text with high semantic fidelity can be useful in multiple applications like enterprise architecture visualization, AI-driven software design, and educational content creation. Hence, in this paper, we focus on leveraging language models to perform semantic understanding of the input text description to generate intermediate code that can be processed to generate high-fidelity architecture diagrams. Unfortunately, no clean large-scale open-access dataset exists, implying lack of any effective open models for this task. Hence, we contribute a comprehensive dataset, \system, comprising scientific architecture images, their corresponding textual descriptions, and associated DOT code representations. Leveraging this resource, we fine-tune a suite of small language models, and also perform in-context learning using GPT-4o. Through extensive experimentation, we show that \system{} models significantly outperform existing baseline models like DiagramAgent and perform at par with in-context learning-based generations from GPT-4o. We make the code, data and models publicly available.

</details>


### [53] [Explain the Flag: Contextualizing Hate Speech Beyond Censorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14970)
*Jason Liartis,Eirini Kaldeli,Lambrini Gyftokosta,Eleftherios Chelioudakis,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种结合LLM与定制词汇表的混合方法，用于检测和解释英语、法语、希腊语中的仇恨言论，通过双管道系统识别固有贬义表达和群体针对性内容，生成有依据的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仇恨言论检测系统主要关注审查或删除，缺乏透明度并可能限制言论自由，且未能解释内容为何有害。需要更透明、可问责且信息丰富的检测方法。

Method: 开发混合方法：结合大型语言模型与三个新创建和整理的词汇表。采用双管道系统：1) 使用定制词汇表检测和消歧问题术语；2) 利用LLM作为上下文感知的群体针对性内容评估器。将输出融合为有依据的解释。

Result: 人类评估表明该混合方法准确且能生成高质量解释，优于仅使用LLM的基线方法。系统能有效识别与身份特征相关的固有贬义表达和直接群体针对性内容。

Conclusion: 混合方法为仇恨言论检测提供了更透明、可问责的解决方案，通过生成解释性输出弥补了传统审查方法的不足，在多语言环境中表现出色。

Abstract: Hate, derogatory, and offensive speech remains a persistent challenge in online platforms and public discourse. While automated detection systems are widely used, most focus on censorship or removal, raising concerns for transparency and freedom of expression, and limiting opportunities to explain why content is harmful. To address these issues, explanatory approaches have emerged as a promising solution, aiming to make hate speech detection more transparent, accountable, and informative. In this paper, we present a hybrid approach that combines Large Language Models (LLMs) with three newly created and curated vocabularies to detect and explain hate speech in English, French, and Greek. Our system captures both inherently derogatory expressions tied to identity characteristics and direct group-targeted content through two complementary pipelines: one that detects and disambiguates problematic terms using the curated vocabularies, and one that leverages LLMs as context-aware evaluators of group-targeting content. The outputs are fused into grounded explanations that clarify why content is flagged. Human evaluation shows that our hybrid approach is accurate, with high-quality explanations, outperforming LLM-only baselines.

</details>


### [54] [IUQ: Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification for Long-Form Large Language Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15109)
*Haozhi Fan,Jinhao Duan,Kaidi Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IUQ框架通过利用样本间一致性和样本内忠实性来量化长文本LLM输出的不确定性，解决了LLM生成中语义连贯但事实不准确的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM发展迅速，但长文本生成中的不确定性量化仍然是一个挑战。现有方法通常限制LLM产生简短或受限的答案集，而许多实际应用需要长文本和自由文本生成。主要困难在于LLM经常产生语义连贯但事实不准确的响应，同时底层语义是多方面的，语言结构复杂。

Method: 提出了Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification (IUQ)框架，利用样本间一致性和样本内忠实性来量化长文本LLM输出的不确定性。采用"询问-响应"范式，提供声明级不确定性和模型忠实性的可靠度量。

Result: 在不同模型系列和模型大小上的实验结果表明，IUQ在两个广泛使用的长文本生成数据集上表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: IUQ框架有效解决了长文本LLM生成中的不确定性量化问题，通过结合样本间一致性和样本内忠实性，为长文本生成提供了可靠的不确定性度量方法。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs), uncertainty quantification in LLM generation is a persistent challenge. Although recent approaches have achieved strong performance by restricting LLMs to produce short or constrained answer sets, many real-world applications require long-form and free-form text generation. A key difficulty in this setting is that LLMs often produce responses that are semantically coherent yet factually inaccurate, while the underlying semantics are multifaceted and the linguistic structure is complex. To tackle this challenge, this paper introduces Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification (IUQ), a novel framework that leverages inter-sample consistency and intra-sample faithfulness to quantify the uncertainty in long-form LLM outputs. By utilizing an interrogate-then-respond paradigm, our method provides reliable measures of claim-level uncertainty and the model's faithfulness. Experimental results across diverse model families and model sizes demonstrate the superior performance of IUQ over two widely used long-form generation datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/louisfanhz/IUQ.

</details>


### [55] [DiscoTrace: Representing and Comparing Answering Strategies of Humans and LLMs in Information-Seeking Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15140)
*Neha Srikanth,Jordan Boyd-Graber,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiscoTrace是一种识别回答者在回应信息寻求问题时使用的修辞策略的方法，通过将答案表示为问题相关话语行为序列来分析不同社区的回答模式，发现LLM缺乏修辞多样性且倾向于过度回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在理解不同人类社区在回答信息寻求问题时使用的修辞策略差异，并评估大型语言模型是否能够模仿这种多样性，以指导开发更具语用能力的LLM回答系统。

Method: 提出DiscoTrace方法，将答案表示为问题相关话语行为序列，结合原始问题的解释，并在修辞结构理论解析的基础上进行标注。该方法应用于九个不同人类社区的答案分析。

Result: 研究发现：1）不同人类社区在答案构建上有明显的偏好多样性；2）LLM在回答中缺乏修辞多样性，即使被提示模仿特定人类社区的指导方针；3）LLM系统性地选择广度优先策略，回答了人类回答者选择不涉及的问题解释。

Conclusion: 研究结果可为开发考虑上下文并借鉴多种策略的语用型LLM回答系统提供指导，帮助LLM更好地适应不同社区的回答风格和需求。

Abstract: We introduce DiscoTrace, a method to identify the rhetorical strategies that answerers use when responding to information-seeking questions. DiscoTrace represents answers as a sequence of question-related discourse acts paired with interpretations of the original question, annotated on top of rhetorical structure theory parses. Applying DiscoTrace to answers from nine different human communities reveals that communities have diverse preferences for answer construction. In contrast, LLMs do not exhibit rhetorical diversity in their answers, even when prompted to mimic specific human community answering guidelines. LLMs also systematically opt for breadth, addressing interpretations of questions that human answerers choose not to address. Our findings can guide the development of pragmatic LLM answerers that consider a range of strategies informed by context in QA.

</details>


### [56] [Compressing Sequences in the Latent Embedding Space: $K$-Token Merging for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15153)
*Zihao Xu,John Harvill,Ziwei Fan,Yizhou Sun,Hao Ding,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: K-Token Merging：一种在潜在嵌入空间压缩长提示的框架，通过将K个连续token嵌入合并为单个嵌入，实现高达75%的输入长度减少，性能损失最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型处理长提示时计算和内存成本高昂，因为自注意力机制随输入长度呈二次方增长。现有提示压缩方法主要在token空间操作，忽略了潜在嵌入空间的低效性。

Method: 提出K-Token Merging框架：1）将连续K个token嵌入块通过轻量级编码器合并为单个嵌入；2）压缩序列由LoRA适配的LLM处理；3）生成仍在原始词汇表中进行。

Result: 在结构推理（Textualized Tree）、情感分类（Amazon Reviews）和代码编辑（CommitPackFT）任务上的实验表明，K-Token Merging在性能与压缩比之间达到帕累托最优，实现高达75%的输入长度减少且性能下降最小。

Conclusion: K-Token Merging通过潜在嵌入空间压缩有效解决了LLM处理长提示的计算效率问题，在保持性能的同时显著减少输入长度，为长上下文处理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) incur significant computational and memory costs when processing long prompts, as full self-attention scales quadratically with input length. Token compression aims to address this challenge by reducing the number of tokens representing inputs. However, existing prompt-compression approaches primarily operate in token space and overlook inefficiencies in the latent embedding space. In this paper, we propose K-Token Merging, a latent-space compression framework that merges each contiguous block of K token embeddings into a single embedding via a lightweight encoder. The compressed sequence is processed by a LoRA-adapted LLM, while generation remains in the original vocabulary. Experiments on structural reasoning (Textualized Tree), sentiment classification (Amazon Reviews), and code editing (CommitPackFT) show that K-Token Merging lies on the Pareto frontier of performance vs. compression, achieving up to 75% input length reduction with minimal performance degradation.

</details>


### [57] [Fabricator or dynamic translator?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15165)
*Lisa Vasileva,Karin Sim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文探讨了LLM在机器翻译中的过度生成现象，包括自我解释、风险性虚构和适当解释等不同类型，并提出了在商业环境中检测和分类这些过度生成的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在机器翻译中表现出色，但由于其生成性质，有时会以各种方式过度生成内容。这些过度生成与NMT中的神经胡言乱语不同，范围从LLM的自我解释、风险性虚构到适当的解释，其中LLM能够像人类翻译者一样工作，为目标受众提供更好的理解。检测和确定过度生成的确切性质是一个具有挑战性的任务。

Method: 论文详细描述了在商业环境中探索的不同策略，包括检测和分类LLM在机器翻译中过度生成的方法。虽然没有具体说明技术细节，但暗示了针对商业应用场景的实用方法。

Result: 论文展示了在商业环境中应用这些策略的结果，表明能够有效检测和分类LLM在翻译中的过度生成现象。

Conclusion: LLM在机器翻译中的过度生成是一个复杂现象，需要专门的检测和分类策略。在商业环境中，这些策略能够帮助识别不同类型的过度生成，包括有益的翻译解释和需要避免的风险性虚构。

Abstract: LLMs are proving to be adept at machine translation although due to their generative nature they may at times overgenerate in various ways. These overgenerations are different from the neurobabble seen in NMT and range from LLM self-explanations, to risky confabulations, to appropriate explanations, where the LLM is able to act as a human translator would, enabling greater comprehension for the target audience. Detecting and determining the exact nature of the overgenerations is a challenging task. We detail different strategies we have explored for our work in a commercial setting, and present our results.

</details>


### [58] [From Tokens to Steps: Verification-Aware Speculative Decoding for Efficient Multi-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15244)
*Kiran Purohit,Ramasuri Narayanam,Soumyabrata Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpecGuard：一种验证感知的推测解码框架，通过模型内部信号进行步骤级验证，提高准确率3.6%并降低延迟约11%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测解码的token中心特性导致错误步骤传播，现有方法使用外部奖励模型但带来额外延迟、计算开销和泛化性限制

Method: 提出SpecGuard框架：1) 采样多个草稿候选并选择最一致的步骤；2) 使用两种轻量级模型内部信号验证：基于注意力的接地分数和基于对数概率的置信度分数；3) 联合决定接受步骤或使用目标模型重新计算

Result: 在多个推理基准测试中，SpecGuard相比传统推测解码和奖励引导的推测解码，准确率提高3.6%，延迟降低约11%

Conclusion: SpecGuard通过仅使用模型内部信号进行步骤级验证，有效解决了推测解码中的错误传播问题，在保持效率的同时显著提升了推理准确性

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by allowing a lightweight draft model to propose outputs that a stronger target model verifies. However, its token-centric nature allows erroneous steps to propagate. Prior approaches mitigate this using external reward models, but incur additional latency, computational overhead, and limit generalizability. We propose SpecGuard, a verification-aware speculative decoding framework that performs step-level verification using only model-internal signals. At each step, SpecGuard samples multiple draft candidates and selects the most consistent step, which is then validated using an ensemble of two lightweight model-internal signals: (i) an attention-based grounding score that measures attribution to the input and previously accepted steps, and (ii) a log-probability-based score that captures token-level confidence. These signals jointly determine whether a step is accepted or recomputed using the target, allocating compute selectively. Experiments across a range of reasoning benchmarks show that SpecGuard improves accuracy by 3.6% while reducing latency by ~11%, outperforming both SD and reward-guided SD.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [59] [Knowledge Graph RAG: Agentic Crawling and Graph Construction in Enterprise Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14220)
*Koushik Chakraborty,Koyel Guha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于Agentic知识图谱和递归爬取的方法，用于解决企业文档生态系统中语义搜索的局限性，相比传统RAG系统在复杂监管查询中实现了70%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG管道在复杂企业文档生态系统中存在局限性，无法有效捕获层次化和互连信息，导致检索不准确，特别是在处理具有继承逻辑和多跳引用的文档时表现不佳。

Method: 提出了Agentic知识图谱方法，采用递归爬取技术来导航文档间的继承逻辑和多跳引用，构建能够捕捉复杂关系的知识图谱结构。

Result: 使用《联邦法规法典》(CFR)进行基准评估，结果显示这种知识图谱增强方法相比标准基于向量的RAG系统实现了70%的准确率提升，能够为复杂监管查询提供详尽且精确的答案。

Conclusion: Agentic知识图谱结合递归爬取是解决复杂企业文档检索问题的有效方法，显著提高了对层次化和互连信息的捕获能力，为复杂监管查询提供了更精确的解决方案。

Abstract: This research paper addresses the limitations of semantic search in complex enterprise document ecosystems. Traditional RAG pipelines often fail to capture hierarchical and interconnected information, leading to retrieval inaccuracies. We propose Agentic Knowledge Graphs featuring Recursive Crawling as a robust solution for navigating superseding logic and multi-hop references. Our benchmark evaluation using the Code of Federal Regulations (CFR) demonstrates that this Knowledge Graph-enhanced approach achieves a 70% accuracy improvement over standard vector-based RAG systems, providing exhaustive and precise answers for complex regulatory queries.

</details>


### [60] [Adaptive Query Routing: A Tier-Based Framework for Hybrid Retrieval Across Financial, Legal, and Medical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14222)
*Afshan Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种检索架构（向量RAG、树推理和自适应混合检索）在金融、法律和医疗领域的表现，发现树推理总体最优，但不同架构在不同查询复杂度层级各有所长，支持开发基于查询复杂度和文档结构的自适应检索系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示向量RAG与基于推理的检索系统各有优势，但缺乏系统性的跨领域比较。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过实现和评估三种检索架构，引入查询复杂度基准，为自适应检索系统开发提供实证依据。

Method: 实现并评估三种检索架构：向量RAG、树推理和提出的自适应混合检索（AHR）；引入四层查询复杂度基准；使用GPT-4驱动的LLM-as-judge评估方法；在金融、法律和医疗领域进行实验；在FinanceBench（150个专家标注的SEC文件问题）上进行验证。

Result: 树推理获得最高总体得分（0.900），但无单一范式在所有层级占优：向量RAG在多文档合成（第4层，得分0.900）表现最佳，混合AHR在交叉引用（0.850）和多章节查询（0.929）表现最优。树推理和混合方法的交叉引用召回率达到100%，而向量搜索仅为91.7%。在FinanceBench验证中，树推理得分0.938，混合AHR 0.901，向量RAG 0.821，树-向量质量差距扩大到11.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明不同检索架构在不同查询类型和文档结构下各有优势，支持开发能够根据查询复杂度和文档结构动态选择策略的自适应检索系统。所有代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become the standard paradigm for grounding Large Language Model outputs in external knowledge. Lumer et al. [1] presented the first systematic evaluation comparing vector-based agentic RAG against hierarchical node-based reasoning systems for financial document QA across 1,200 SEC filings, finding vector-based systems achieved a 68% win rate. Concurrently, the PageIndex framework [2] demonstrated 98.7% accuracy on FinanceBench through purely reasoning-based retrieval. This paper extends their work by: (i) implementing and evaluating three retrieval architectures: Vector RAG, Tree Reasoning, and the proposed Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (AHR) across financial, legal, and medical domains; (ii) introducing a four-tier query complexity benchmark; and (iii) employing GPT-4-powered LLM-as-judge evaluation. Experiments reveal that Tree Reasoning achieves the highest overall score (0.900), but no single paradigm dominates across all tiers: Vector RAG wins on multi-document synthesis (Tier 4, score 0.900), while the Hybrid AHR achieves the best performance on cross-reference (0.850) and multi-section queries (0.929). Cross-reference recall reaches 100% for tree-based and hybrid approaches versus 91.7% for vector search, quantifying a critical capability gap. Validation on FinanceBench (150 expert-annotated questions on real SEC 10-K and 10-Q filings) confirms and strengthens these findings: Tree Reasoning scores 0.938, Hybrid AHR 0.901, and Vector RAG 0.821, with the Tree--Vector quality gap widening to 11.7 percentage points on real-world documents. These findings support the development of adaptive retrieval systems that dynamically select strategies based on query complexity and document structure. All code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [61] [TRACE: A Conversational Framework for Sustainable Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14223)
*Ashmi Banerjee,Adithi Satish,Wolfgang Wörndl,Yashar Deldjoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TRACE是一个基于LLM的多智能体对话式旅游推荐框架，通过交互式引导促进可持续旅游，平衡用户相关性与环境影响，避免强化过度拥挤和碳密集型目的地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对话式旅游推荐系统主要优化用户相关性和便利性，往往强化热门、过度拥挤的旅游目的地和碳密集型旅行选择，缺乏对可持续旅游的考虑。

Method: 采用模块化的编排器-工作者架构，专业智能体负责：1) 引出潜在的可持续性偏好；2) 构建结构化用户画像；3) 生成平衡相关性与环境影响的推荐。关键创新是使用智能体反事实解释和LLM驱动的澄清问题，共同呈现更环保的替代方案并精炼意图理解。

Result: 用户研究和语义对齐分析表明，TRACE有效支持可持续决策制定，同时保持推荐质量和交互响应性。系统已在Google的Agent Development Kit上实现，提供完整代码、Docker设置、提示词和公开演示视频。

Conclusion: TRACE框架通过多智能体架构和交互式引导，成功促进可持续旅游决策，在不强制的情况下培养用户反思，为可持续旅游推荐系统提供了可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional conversational travel recommender systems primarily optimize for user relevance and convenience, often reinforcing popular, overcrowded destinations and carbon-intensive travel choices. To address this, we present TRACE (Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations), a multi-agent, LLM-based framework that promotes sustainable tourism through interactive nudging. TRACE uses a modular orchestrator-worker architecture where specialized agents elicit latent sustainability preferences, construct structured user personas, and generate recommendations that balance relevance with environmental impact.
  A key innovation lies in its use of agentic counterfactual explanations and LLM-driven clarifying questions, which together surface greener alternatives and refine understanding of intent, fostering user reflection without coercion. User studies and semantic alignment analyses demonstrate that TRACE effectively supports sustainable decision-making while preserving recommendation quality and interactive responsiveness. TRACE is implemented on Google's Agent Development Kit, with full code, Docker setup, prompts, and a publicly available demo video to ensure reproducibility.
  A project summary, including all resources, prompts, and demo access, is available at https://ashmibanerjee.github.io/trace-chatbot.

</details>


### [62] [FRESCO: Benchmarking and Optimizing Re-rankers for Evolving Semantic Conflict in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14227)
*Sohyun An,Hayeon Lee,Shuibenyang Yuan,Chun-cheng Jason Chen,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Vijai Mohan,Alexander Min*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FRESCO基准测试评估检索增强生成中的重排序器在动态信息环境下的表现，发现现有重排序器偏向过时但语义丰富的文档，并提出指令优化框架改善此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排序器基准主要在静态环境下评估，无法充分评估在信息演化场景下的性能，而现实系统经常需要在时间上不同的证据中进行选择，这是一个关键的研究空白。

Method: 引入FRESCO基准，通过将时效性查询与历史维基百科修订配对，测试重排序器能否优先考虑事实性最新证据同时保持语义相关性；并研究指令优化框架来缓解问题。

Result: 评估显示现有重排序器存在一致的失败模式：强烈偏向较旧但语义丰富的文档，即使这些文档事实已过时；通过识别帕累托最优指令，在演化知识任务上获得高达27%的增益，同时在非演化知识任务上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: FRESCO基准填补了重排序器在动态信息环境下评估的空白，揭示了现有方法的局限性，并展示了指令优化在平衡时效性和语义相关性方面的有效性，为改进检索增强生成系统提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is a key approach to mitigating the temporal staleness of large language models (LLMs) by grounding responses in up-to-date evidence. Within the RAG pipeline, re-rankers play a pivotal role in selecting the most useful documents from retrieved candidates. However, existing benchmarks predominantly evaluate re-rankers in static settings and do not adequately assess performance under evolving information -- a critical gap, as real-world systems often must choose among temporally different pieces of evidence. To address this limitation, we introduce FRESCO (Factual Recency and Evolving Semantic COnflict), a benchmark for evaluating re-rankers in temporally dynamic contexts. By pairing recency-seeking queries with historical Wikipedia revisions, FRESCO tests whether re-rankers can prioritize factually recent evidence while maintaining semantic relevance. Our evaluation reveals a consistent failure mode across existing re-rankers: a strong bias toward older, semantically rich documents, even when they are factually obsolete. We further investigate an instruction optimization framework to mitigate this issue. By identifying Pareto-optimal instructions that balance Evolving and Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks, we obtain gains of up to 27% on Evolving Knowledge tasks while maintaining competitive performance on Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks.

</details>


### [63] [Controlling Authority Retrieval: A Missing Retrieval Objective for Authority-Governed Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14488)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出控制性权威检索（CAR）问题，形式化定义权威文档集合的活跃前沿检索，证明理论边界，并通过三个真实领域数据集验证两阶段检索方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有正式权威性的知识领域（如法律、药品监管、软件安全），后续文档可以正式废止先前文档，但语义上可能相距甚远。传统基于语义相似度的检索无法正确处理这种权威控制关系，导致检索结果不准确。

Method: 1. 形式化定义控制性权威检索（CAR）问题：检索权威闭包的活跃前沿front(cl(A_k(q)))；2. 提出定理4（CAR正确性特征）：给出检索集R满足TCA(R,q)=1的充要条件；3. 提出命题2（范围可识别性上界）：建立phi(q)作为硬性最坏情况上限；4. 设计两阶段检索方法：第一阶段检索语义相关文档，第二阶段识别权威控制关系；5. 在三个真实数据集（安全公告、最高法院推翻案例、FDA药品记录）上验证方法。

Result: 1. 理论结果：定理4提供CAR正确性的形式化特征，命题2证明phi(q)是范围索引算法的硬性上限；2. 实证结果：两阶段检索显著优于密集检索：安全公告（密集TCA@5=0.270，两阶段0.975）、最高法院案例（密集0.172，两阶段0.926）、FDA记录（密集0.064，两阶段0.774）；3. GPT-4o-mini实验：密集RAG在39%存在补丁的查询中产生"未修补"错误声明，两阶段降至16%。

Conclusion: 控制性权威检索是一个与语义相似度检索不同的数学问题，需要专门的形式化处理。两阶段检索方法能有效解决权威文档的废止关系识别问题，显著提高检索准确性，减少下游应用中的错误声明。研究提供了理论框架、基准数据集和评估工具。

Abstract: In any domain where knowledge accumulates under formal authority -- law, drug regulation, software security -- a later document can formally void an earlier one while remaining semantically distant from it. We formalize this as Controlling Authority Retrieval (CAR): recovering the active frontier front(cl(A_k(q))) of the authority closure of the semantic anchor set -- a different mathematical problem from argmax_d s(q,d). The two central results are: Theorem 4 (CAR-Correctness Characterization) gives necessary-and-sufficient conditions on any retrieved set R for TCA(R,q)=1 -- frontier inclusion and no-ignored-superseder -- independent of how R was produced. Proposition 2 (Scope Identifiability Upper Bound) establishes phi(q) as a hard worst-case ceiling: for any scope-indexed algorithm, TCA@k <= phi(q) * R_anchor(q), proved by an adversarial permutation argument. Three independent real-world corpora validate the proved structure: security advisories (Dense TCA@5=0.270, two-stage 0.975), SCOTUS overruling pairs (Dense=0.172, two-stage 0.926), FDA drug records (Dense=0.064, two-stage 0.774). A GPT-4o-mini experiment shows the downstream cost: Dense RAG produces explicit "not patched" claims for 39% of queries where a patch exists; Two-Stage cuts this to 16%. Four benchmark datasets, domain adapters, and a single-command scorer are released at https://github.com/andremir/car-retrieval.

</details>


### [64] [NewsTorch: A PyTorch-based Toolkit for Learner-oriented News Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14510)
*Rongyao Wang,Veronica Liesaputra,Zhiyi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NewsTorch是一个基于PyTorch的新闻推荐系统工具包，旨在为学习者提供模块化、可扩展的框架和用户友好的GUI平台，支持数据集处理、模型训练和标准化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏专门面向学习者的新闻推荐工具包，这阻碍了新闻推荐领域的研究进展。需要提供一个既能帮助学习者理解概念又能获得实践经验的工具。

Method: 开发了一个基于PyTorch的模块化、解耦且可扩展的框架，包含学习者友好的GUI平台，支持数据集下载和预处理，以及最先进的神经新闻推荐模型的训练、验证和测试。

Result: NewsTorch工具包已在GitHub上开源发布，提供了完整的新闻推荐研究环境，支持标准化评估指标，确保公平比较和可重复实验。

Conclusion: NewsTorch填补了新闻推荐领域缺乏专门学习者工具包的空白，为研究人员和学习者提供了一个全面、易用且可扩展的平台，有助于推动新闻推荐研究的进展。

Abstract: News recommender systems are devised to alleviate the information overload, attracting more and more researchers' attention in recent years. The lack of a dedicated learner-oriented news recommendation toolkit hinders the advancement of research in news recommendation. We propose a PyTorch-based news recommendation toolkit called NewsTorch, developed to support learners in acquiring both conceptual understanding and practical experience. This toolkit provides a modular, decoupled, and extensible framework with a learner-friendly GUI platform that supports dataset downloading and preprocessing. It also enables training, validation, and testing of state-of-the-art neural news recommendation models with standardized evaluation metrics, ensuring fair comparison and reproducible experiments. Our open-source toolkit is released on Github: https://github.com/whonor/NewsTorch.

</details>


### [65] [CPGRec+: A Balance-oriented Framework for Personalized Video Game Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14586)
*Xiping Li,Aier Yang,Jianghong Ma,Kangzhe Liu,Shanshan Feng,Haijun Zhang,Yi Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CPGRec+通过偏好感知边重加权和LLM增强表示生成，解决游戏推荐中准确性与多样性的权衡问题，同时缓解GNN过平滑问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN游戏推荐系统过于注重准确性而忽视多样性，且未考虑玩家-游戏交互的重要程度差异，同时未能充分利用LLM的推理能力来缓解GNN过平滑问题。

Method: 提出两个新模块：1) 偏好感知边重加权(PER)模块，为边分配带符号权重以区分重要兴趣与不兴趣，并量化偏好强度缓解过平滑；2) 偏好感知表示生成(PRG)模块，利用LLM通过比较全局与个人兴趣生成游戏和玩家的情境化描述，优化表示学习。

Result: 在两个Steam数据集上的实验表明，CPGRec+在准确性和多样性方面均优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: CPGRec+通过结合偏好感知边重加权和LLM增强表示生成，有效解决了游戏推荐中准确性与多样性的权衡问题，并缓解了GNN的过平滑问题。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of gaming industry requires advanced recommender systems tailored to its dynamic landscape. Existing Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based methods primarily prioritize accuracy over diversity, overlooking their inherent trade-off. To address this, we previously proposed CPGRec, a balance-oriented gaming recommender system. However, CPGRec fails to account for critical disparities in player-game interactions, which carry varying significance in reflecting players' personal preferences and may exacerbate over-smoothness issues inherent in GNN-based models. Moreover, existing approaches underutilize the reasoning capabilities and extensive knowledge of large language models (LLMs) in addressing these limitations. To bridge this gap, we propose two new modules. First, Preference-informed Edge Reweighting (PER) module assigns signed edge weights to qualitatively distinguish significant player interests and disinterests while then quantitatively measuring preference strength to mitigate over-smoothing in graph convolutions. Second, Preference-informed Representation Generation (PRG) module leverages LLMs to generate contextualized descriptions of games and players by reasoning personal preferences from comparing global and personal interests, thereby refining representations of players and games. Experiments on \textcolor{black}{two Steam datasets} demonstrate CPGRec+'s superior accuracy and diversity over state-of-the-art models. The code is accessible at https://github.com/HsipingLi/CPGRec-Plus.

</details>


### [66] [Category-based and Popularity-guided Video Game Recommendation: A Balance-oriented Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14598)
*Xiping Li,Jianghong Ma,Kangzhe Liu,Shanshan Feng,Haijun Zhang,Yutong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CPGRec是一个新颖的视频游戏推荐框架，通过三个模块（准确性驱动、多样性驱动和综合模块）平衡推荐准确性和多样性，在Steam数据集上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频游戏推荐系统存在三个主要问题：1）过度关注准确性而忽视多样性，导致推荐结果单一；2）现有方法缺乏在游戏之间建立严格连接以提升准确性；3）许多关注多样性的方法未能充分利用项目类别和流行度等关键信息。

Method: 提出CPGRec框架，包含三个模块：1）准确性驱动模块：通过更严格的方式连接游戏，增强现有准确性导向方法的推荐精度；2）多样性驱动模块：在游戏图中连接不同类别的邻居，利用流行游戏节点放大长尾游戏在玩家-游戏二分图中的影响力；3）综合模块：结合前两个模块，采用新的负样本评分重加权方法来平衡准确性和多样性。

Result: 在Steam数据集上的实验结果表明，CPGRec方法在提升游戏推荐效果方面具有有效性，能够同时改善推荐的准确性和多样性。

Conclusion: CPGRec框架成功解决了当前视频游戏推荐系统中准确性与多样性之间的平衡问题，通过创新的模块设计和信息利用策略，为游戏推荐提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, the video game industry has experienced substantial growth, presenting players with a vast array of game choices. This surge in options has spurred the need for a specialized recommender system tailored for video games. However, current video game recommendation approaches tend to prioritize accuracy over diversity, potentially leading to unvaried game suggestions. In addition, the existing game recommendation methods commonly lack the ability to establish strict connections between games to enhance accuracy. Furthermore, many existing diversity-focused methods fail to leverage crucial item information, such as item category and popularity during neighbor modeling and message propagation. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework, called CPGRec, comprising three modules, namely accuracy-driven, diversity-driven, and comprehensive modules. The first module extends the state-of-the-art accuracy-focused game recommendation method by connecting games in a more stringent manner to enhance recommendation accuracy. The second module connects neighbors with diverse categories within the proposed game graph and harnesses the advantages of popular game nodes to amplify the influence of long-tail games within the player-game bipartite graph, thereby enriching recommendation diversity. The third module combines the above two modules and employs a new negative-sample rating score reweighting method to balance accuracy and diversity. Experimental results on the Steam dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method in improving game recommendations. The dataset and source codes are anonymously released at: https://github.com/CPGRec2024/CPGRec.git.

</details>


### [67] [Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14613)
*Xiangrui Xiong,Hang Liang,Baiyang Chen,Zifei Pan,Yanli Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: U-GLAD：一种不确定性感知的生成式学习路径推荐框架，通过高斯LSTM建模认知状态不确定性，使用目标导向概念编码器对齐学习目标，采用扩散模型生成最优概念表示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习路径推荐方法存在两个主要问题：1）未能考虑历史交互中的不确定性（如侥幸猜对或意外失误）；2）缺乏对不同学习目标的适应性。这导致推荐结果不够准确和个性化。

Method: 提出U-GLAD框架：1）使用高斯LSTM将认知状态建模为概率分布，捕捉学习者的真实认知状态；2）设计目标导向概念编码器，利用多头注意力和目标特定变换动态对齐概念语义与个体学习目标；3）采用生成式扩散模型预测下一个最优概念的潜在表示，而非传统判别式排序方法。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的广泛评估表明，U-GLAD显著优于代表性基线方法。进一步分析证实了其在感知交互不确定性和提供稳定、目标驱动的推荐路径方面的卓越能力。

Conclusion: U-GLAD通过建模认知状态不确定性、目标对齐概念编码和生成式扩散预测，实现了更准确、个性化的学习路径推荐，解决了现有方法在不确定性和目标适应性方面的局限性。

Abstract: Learning Path Recommendation (LPR) is critical for personalized education, yet current methods often fail to account for historical interaction uncertainty (e.g., lucky guesses or accidental slips) and lack adaptability to diverse learning goals. We propose U-GLAD (Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion). To address representation bias, the framework models cognitive states as probability distributions, capturing the learner's underlying true state via a Gaussian LSTM. To ensure highly personalized recommendation, a goal-oriented concept encoder utilizes multi-head attention and objective-specific transformations to dynamically align concept semantics with individual learning goals, generating uniquely tailored embeddings. Unlike traditional discriminative ranking approaches, our model employs a generative diffusion model to predict the latent representation of the next optimal concept. Extensive evaluations on three public datasets demonstrate that U-GLAD significantly outperforms representative baselines. Further analyses confirm its superior capability in perceiving interaction uncertainty and providing stable, goal-driven recommendation paths.

</details>


### [68] [Federated User Behavior Modeling for Privacy-Preserving LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14833)
*Lei Guo,Hongyun Yang,Pengjie Ren,Tong Chen,Hui Liu,Zhumin Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SF-UBM：一种语义增强的联邦用户行为建模方法，用于解决非重叠隐私保护跨域推荐问题，通过自然语言作为通用桥梁连接不相交的领域，结合知识蒸馏和对齐技术提升LLM在推荐系统中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在推荐系统中面临用户数据稀疏性限制，跨域推荐方法通常假设领域重叠且可访问，缺乏对隐私保护跨域推荐（PPCDR）的关注。非重叠PPCDR面临三大挑战：1)无法跨域共享用户身份或行为数据；2)跨域数据模态异质性；3)传统推荐模型与LLM特征空间不匹配。

Method: 提出SF-UBM方法：1)使用自然语言作为通用桥梁，通过语义增强的联邦架构连接不相交领域，加密共享基于文本的物品表示，用户数据保持本地；2)设计事实-反事实知识蒸馏模块，整合领域无关和领域特定知识；3)将预学习的用户偏好和跨域物品表示投影到软提示空间，对齐行为和语义空间。

Result: 在三个真实世界领域对上进行了广泛实验，实验结果表明SF-UBM相比最近的SOTA方法具有显著有效性。

Conclusion: SF-UBM成功解决了非重叠隐私保护跨域推荐中的三大挑战，通过语义增强的联邦架构、知识蒸馏和空间对齐技术，有效提升了LLM在推荐系统中的性能，为隐私敏感的跨域推荐提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models have shown great success in recommender systems. However, the limited and sparse nature of user data often restricts the LLM's ability to effectively model behavior patterns. To address this, existing studies have explored cross-domain solutions by conducting Cross-Domain Recommendation tasks. But previous methods typically assume domains are overlapped and can be accessed readily. None of the LLM methods address the privacy-preserving issues in the CDR settings, that is, Privacy-Preserving Cross-Domain Recommendation. Conducting non-overlapping PPCDR with LLM is challenging since: 1)The inability to share user identity or behavioral data across domains impedes effective cross-domain alignment. 2)The heterogeneity of data modalities across domains complicates knowledge integration. 3)Fusing collaborative filtering signals from traditional recommendation models with LLMs is difficult, as they operate within distinct feature spaces. To address the above issues, we propose SF-UBM, a Semantic-enhanced Federated User Behavior Modeling method. Specifically, to deal with Challenge 1, we leverage natural language as a universal bridge to connect disjoint domains via a semantic-enhanced federated architecture. Here, text-based item representations are encrypted and shared, while user-specific data remains local. To handle Challenge 2, we design a Fact-counter Knowledge Distillation module to integrate domain-agnostic knowledge with domain-specific knowledge, across different data modalities. To tackle Challenge 3, we project pre-learned user preferences and cross-domain item representations into the soft prompt space, aligning behavioral and semantic spaces for effective LLM learning. We conduct extensive experiments on three pairs of real-world domains, and the experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of SF-UBM compared to the recent SOTA methods.

</details>


### [69] [Well Begun is Half Done: Training-Free and Model-Agnostic Semantically Guaranteed User Representation Initialization for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14839)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Hewei Wang,Jianheng Tang,Wei Wang,Xiping Hu,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SG-URInit方法，通过整合用户交互项目的模态特征和对应聚类的全局特征，为多模态推荐中的用户表示提供语义丰富的初始化，弥补项目与用户表示之间的语义鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐方法忽视了用户表示初始化的关键作用。项目自然关联丰富的模态信息，而用户缺乏这种固有信息，导致基于模态信息初始化的项目表示与随机初始化的用户表示之间存在显著的语义鸿沟。

Method: 提出语义保证的用户表示初始化方法SG-URInit。为每个用户构建初始表示时，整合两个部分：1）用户交互过的项目的模态特征（局部语义）；2）这些项目对应聚类的全局特征（全局语义）。该方法无需训练且模型无关，可无缝集成到现有多模态推荐模型中。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，将SG-URInit集成到先进的多模态推荐模型中能显著提升推荐性能。此外，该方法还能进一步缓解项目冷启动问题，并加速模型收敛。

Conclusion: SG-URInit是一种高效实用的多模态推荐解决方案，通过语义丰富的用户表示初始化，有效弥补了项目与用户表示之间的语义鸿沟，提升了推荐性能并解决了冷启动问题。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal recommendations, which leverage diverse modality information to mitigate data sparsity and improve recommendation accuracy, have gained significant attention. However, existing multimodal recommendations overlook the critical role of user representation initialization. Unlike items, which are naturally associated with rich modality information, users lack such inherent information. Consequently, item representations initialized based on meaningful modality information and user representations initialized randomly exhibit a significant semantic gap.
  To this end, we propose a Semantically Guaranteed User Representation Initialization (SG-URInit). SG-URInit constructs the initial representation for each user by integrating both the modality features of the items they have interacted with and the global features of their corresponding clusters. SG-URInit enables the initialization of semantically enriched user representations that effectively capture both local (item-level) and global (cluster-level) semantics.
  Our SG-URInit is training-free and model-agnostic, meaning it can be seamlessly integrated into existing multimodal recommendation models without incurring any additional computational overhead during training. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that incorporating SG-URInit into advanced multimodal recommendation models significantly enhances recommendation performance. Furthermore, the results show that SG-URInit can further alleviate the item cold-start problem and also accelerate model convergence, making it an efficient and practical solution for multimodal recommendations.

</details>


### [70] [GenRec: A Preference-Oriented Generative Framework for Large-Scale Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14878)
*Yanyan Zou,Junbo Qi,Lunsong Huang,Yu Li,Kewei Xu,Jiabao Gao,Binglei Zhao,Xuanhua Yang,Sulong Xu,Shengjie Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GenRec是一个部署在京东App上的偏好导向生成推荐框架，通过页面级NTP训练、语义ID压缩和GRPO-SR强化学习，解决了生成式推荐在工业规模部署中的三大挑战，实现了显著的线上效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生成式推荐在大规模工业系统中面临的三个核心挑战：1) 分页请求机制下相同模型输入产生不一致输出；2) 基于语义ID的多token项目表示编码长用户行为序列的成本过高；3) 生成策略与细微用户偏好信号的对齐问题。

Method: 提出GenRec框架，包含三个关键技术：1) 页面级NTP训练任务，监督整个交互页面而非单个项目；2) 非对称线性Token Merger压缩语义ID，减少输入长度约2倍；3) GRPO-SR强化学习方法，结合群组相对策略优化和NLL正则化，使用混合奖励（密集奖励模型+相关性门控）缓解奖励攻击。

Result: 在为期一个月的在线A/B测试中，GenRec相比现有流水线实现了点击量提升9.5%，交易量提升8.7%的显著改进。

Conclusion: GenRec成功解决了生成式推荐在工业规模部署中的关键挑战，通过统一的解码器架构实现了性能显著提升，证明了其在真实生产环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) offers a promising paradigm for recommendation through next-token prediction (NTP). However, scaling it to large-scale industrial systems introduces three challenges: (i) within a single request, the identical model inputs may produce inconsistent outputs due to the pagination request mechanism; (ii) the prohibitive cost of encoding long user behavior sequences with multi-token item representations based on semantic IDs, and (iii) aligning the generative policy with nuanced user preference signals. We present GenRec, a preference-oriented generative framework deployed on the JD App that addresses above challenges within a single decoder-only architecture. For training objective, we propose Page-wise NTP task, which supervises over an entire interaction page rather than each interacted item individually, providing denser gradient signal and resolving the one-to-many ambiguity of point-wise training. On the prefilling side, an asymmetric linear Token Merger compresses multi-token Semantic IDs in the prompt while preserving full-resolution decoding, reducing input length by ~2X with negligible accuracy loss. To further align outputs with user satisfaction, we introduce GRPO-SR, a reinforcement learning method that pairs Group Relative Policy Optimization with NLL regularization for training stability, and employs Hybrid Rewards combining a dense reward model with a relevance gate to mitigate reward hacking. In month-long online A/B tests serving production traffic, GenRec achieves 9.5% improvement in click count and 8.7% in transaction count over the existing pipeline.

</details>


### [71] [Metric-agnostic Learning-to-Rank via Boosting and Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15101)
*Camilo Gomez,Pengyang Wang,Yanjie Fu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种新的列表式学习排序框架，通过可微排序损失函数解决传统方法对单一排序指标的依赖问题，实现更高效和泛化的排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习排序方法存在两个主要问题：1）直接优化排序指标是非可微的，导致训练过程不稳定和低效；2）依赖单一排序指标限制了方法在其他相关指标上的泛化能力。

Method: 提出新的列表式LTR框架，包含：1）结合排序算子平滑近似和每查询平均均方损失的可微排序损失函数；2）采用梯度提升机来最小化每个列表的损失。

Result: 大量实验结果表明，该方法在信息检索指标上优于当前最先进方法，同时保持相似的效率。

Conclusion: 提出的框架通过可微排序损失解决了传统LTR方法的局限性，实现了更高效和泛化的排序性能，为学习排序领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning-to-Rank (LTR) is a supervised machine learning approach that constructs models specifically designed to order a set of items or documents based on their relevance or importance to a given query or context. Despite significant success in real-world information retrieval systems, current LTR methods rely on one prefix ranking metric (e.g., such as Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain (NDCG) or Mean Average Precision (MAP)) for optimizing the ranking objective function. Such metric-dependent setting limits LTR methods from two perspectives: (1) non-differentiable problem: directly optimizing ranking functions over a given ranking metric is inherently non-smooth, making the training process unstable and inefficient; (2) limited ranking utility: optimizing over one single metric makes it difficult to generalize well to other ranking metrics of interest. To address the above issues, we propose a novel listwise LTR framework for efficient and generalizable ranking purpose. Specifically, we propose a new differentiable ranking loss that combines a smooth approximation to the ranking operator with the average mean square loss per query. Then, we adapt gradient-boosting machines to minimize our proposed loss with respect to each list, a novel contribution. Finally, extensive experimental results confirm that our method outperforms the current state-of-the-art in information retrieval measures with similar efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [Simulating Human Cognition: Heartbeat-Driven Autonomous Thinking Activity Scheduling for LLM-based AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14178)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于心跳驱动的自主思维活动调度机制，使LLM智能体能够进行主动、自适应和持续的自我调节，通过周期性调度动态认知模块来提升智能体的适应性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体通常采用固定的流程或失败触发的反思机制，导致控制流程僵化、反应性过强，限制了智能体的适应性和效率。智能体往往冲动行事或仅在错误发生后进行修正，缺乏主动、自适应的自我调节能力。

Method: 提出心跳驱动的自主思维活动调度机制：1）采用周期性"心跳"机制协调动态认知模块（如规划器、批评器、回忆器、梦想器）；2）调度器基于时间模式和历史上下文学习何时激活特定思维活动；3）采用功能化方法，允许动态添加或移除认知模块而无需结构重构；4）提出元学习策略进行持续策略适应，调度器利用历史交互日志随时间优化认知策略。

Result: 评估结果表明，该方法能有效基于历史数据学习调度认知活动，并能自主整合新的思维模块。相比传统方法，实现了更主动、自适应和高效的智能体行为控制。

Conclusion: 心跳驱动的自主思维活动调度机制为LLM智能体提供了一种新颖的自我调节框架，通过模拟人类认知的自然节奏，实现了认知模块的动态协调和持续优化，显著提升了智能体的适应性和效率。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in reasoning and tool use, yet they often suffer from rigid, reactive control flows that limit their adaptability and efficiency. Most existing frameworks rely on fixed pipelines or failure-triggered reflection, causing agents to act impulsively or correct errors only after they occur. In this paper, we introduce Heartbeat-Driven Autonomous Thinking Activity Scheduling, a mechanism that enables proactive, adaptive, and continuous self-regulation. Mirroring the natural rhythm of human cognition, our system employs a periodic ``heartbeat'' mechanism to orchestrate a dynamic repertoire of cognitive modules (e.g., Planner, Critic, Recaller, Dreamer). Unlike traditional approaches that rely on hard-coded symbolic rules or immediate reactive triggers, our scheduler learns to determine when to engage specific thinking activities -- such as recalling memories, summarizing experiences, or strategic planning -- based on temporal patterns and historical context. This functional approach allows cognitive modules to be dynamically added or removed without structural reengineering. Meanwhile, we propose a meta-learning strategy for continual policy adaptation, where the scheduler optimizes its cognitive strategy over time using historical interaction logs. Evaluation results demonstrate that our approach effectively learns to schedule cognitive activities based on historical data and can autonomously integrate new thinking modules.

</details>


### [73] [Fun-TSG: A Function-Driven Multivariate Time Series Generator with Variable-Level Anomaly Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14221)
*Pierre Lotte,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fun-TSG是一个完全可定制的时间序列生成器，旨在解决多变量时间序列异常检测评估中的数据集限制问题，提供细粒度标注和透明生成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多变量时间序列异常检测基准数据集存在三个主要问题：缺乏细粒度异常标注、不提供明确的变量间和时序依赖关系、对底层生成机制缺乏洞察。这些限制阻碍了检测模型的开发和严格比较，特别是那些针对可解释和变量特定输出的模型。

Method: 作者提出了Fun-TSG，一个完全可定制的时间序列生成器。该工具支持两种生成模式：1）基于随机采样的依赖结构和异常类型的全自动生成；2）通过用户定义方程和异常配置的手动生成。两种模式都提供数据生成过程的完全透明性，包括变量和时间戳级别的真实异常标签访问。

Result: Fun-TSG能够创建多样化、可解释且可复现的基准测试场景，支持对经典和现代异常检测模型进行细粒度性能分析。该工具提供了数据生成过程的完全透明性，解决了现有基准数据集的关键限制。

Conclusion: Fun-TSG填补了多变量时间序列异常检测评估中的关键空白，通过提供完全可定制、透明且具有细粒度标注的时间序列生成能力，支持更高质量、更严格的模型开发和比较，特别是对于需要可解释和变量特定输出的检测模型。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of anomaly detection methods in multivariate time series remains an open challenge, largely due to the limitations of existing benchmark datasets. Current resources often lack fine-grained anomaly annotations, do not provide explicit intervariable and temporal dependencies, and offer little insight into the underlying generative mechanisms. These shortcomings hinder the development and rigorous comparison of detection models, especially those targeting interpretable and variable-specific outputs. To address this gap, we introduce Fun-TSG, a fully customizable time series generator designed to support high-quality evaluation of anomaly detection systems. Our tool enables both fully automated generation, based on randomly sampled dependency structures and anomaly types, and manual generation through user-defined equations and anomaly configurations. In both cases, it provides full transparency over the data generation process, including access to ground-truth anomaly labels at the variable and timestamp levels. Fun-TSG supports the creation of diverse, interpretable, and reproducible benchmarking scenarios, enabling fine-grained performance analysis for both classical and modern anomaly detection models.

</details>


### [74] [GFT: From Imitation to Reward Fine-Tuning with Unbiased Group Advantages and Dynamic Coefficient Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14258)
*Wangjie Gan,Miao Pan,Linbo Xi,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Jianwei Yin,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GFT提出了一种新的后训练框架，通过分组优势学习和动态系数校正解决SFT的奖励稀疏和优化不稳定问题，实现知识注入与泛化的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练方法（SFT和RL）难以有效统一高效知识注入与鲁棒泛化。研究发现SFT本质上是具有极端稀疏隐式奖励和不稳定逆概率加权的策略梯度优化，导致单一路径依赖、熵崩溃和梯度爆炸。

Method: 提出Group Fine-Tuning (GFT)框架：1) Group Advantage Learning：构建多样化响应组并推导归一化对比监督以缓解奖励稀疏；2) Dynamic Coefficient Rectification：自适应限制逆概率权重以稳定优化，同时保持高效知识注入。

Result: 实验表明GFT持续超越基于SFT的方法，并产生与后续RL训练更平滑集成的策略。

Conclusion: GFT通过诊断SFT的固有局限性并提出系统性解决方案，为LLM后训练提供了更统一、稳定和有效的框架，实现了知识注入与泛化能力的更好平衡。

Abstract: Large language models are typically post-trained using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), yet effectively unifying efficient knowledge injection with robust generalization remains challenging. In this work, we provide a training-dynamics analysis showing that SFT can be interpreted as a special case of policy gradient optimization with an extremely sparse implicit reward and unstable inverse-probability weighting, which together lead to single-path dependency, entropy collapse, and gradient explosion. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Group Fine-Tuning (GFT), a unified post-training framework that addresses these intrinsic limitations through two mechanisms: Group Advantage Learning, which constructs diverse response groups and derives normalized contrastive supervision to alleviate reward sparsity, and Dynamic Coefficient Rectification, which adaptively bounds inverse-probability weights to stabilize optimization while preserving efficient knowledge injection. Experiments demonstrate that GFT consistently surpasses SFT-based methods and yields policies that integrate more smoothly with subsequent RL training.

</details>


### [75] [Seeing Through Experts Eyes A Foundational Vision Language Model Trained on Radiologists Gaze and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14316)
*Kinhei Lee,Peiyuan Jing,Zhenxuan Zhang,Yue Yang,Tao Wang,Dominic C Marshall,Yingying Fang,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GazeX是一个视觉语言模型，利用放射科医生的眼动追踪数据作为行为先验来模拟专家诊断推理，通过结合注视轨迹和固定模式，学习遵循放射科医生注意力的时空结构，在放射学报告生成、疾病定位和视觉问答方面产生更准确、可解释且与专家一致的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模视觉语言模型在胸部X光解读方面存在局限性，模型输出与放射科医生推理之间存在差距。大多数系统仅优化语义信息，未能模拟专家如何视觉检查医学图像，常常忽略关键发现或偏离既定的诊断工作流程。放射科医生遵循结构化协议（如ABCDEF方法）确保系统检查所有临床相关区域，减少遗漏发现并支持可靠的诊断推理。

Method: 引入GazeX视觉语言模型，利用放射科医生的眼动追踪数据作为行为先验来建模专家诊断推理。通过将注视轨迹和固定模式纳入预训练，GazeX学习遵循放射科医生注意力的时空结构，并以临床有意义的序列整合观察结果。使用来自五位放射科医生的超过30,000个注视关键帧的精选数据集进行训练。

Result: GazeX在放射学报告生成、疾病定位和视觉问答方面产生更准确、可解释且与专家一致的输出。模型利用231,835个放射学研究、780,014个问答对和1,162个带有边界框的图像-句子对进行验证。与自主报告系统不同，GazeX产生可验证的证据工件，包括检查轨迹和发现链接的定位区域，支持高效的人工验证和安全的人机协作。

Conclusion: 通过专家眼睛学习为放射学及其他领域提供了实现更可信、可解释和诊断稳健的AI系统的实用途径。GazeX方法通过模拟专家视觉检查过程，弥合了AI输出与临床推理之间的差距，支持可验证的人机协作。

Abstract: Large scale vision language models have shown promise in automating chest Xray interpretation, yet their clinical utility remains limited by a gap between model outputs and radiologist reasoning. Most systems optimize for semantic information without emulating how experts visually examine medical images, often overlooking critical findings or diverging from established diagnostic workflows. Radiologists follow structured protocols (e.g., the ABCDEF approach) that ensure all clinically relevant regions are systematically examined, reducing missed findings and supporting reliable diagnostic reasoning. We introduce GazeX, a vision language model that leverages radiologists' eye tracking data as a behavioral prior to model expert diagnostic reasoning. By incorporating gaze trajectories and fixation patterns into pretraining, GazeX learns to follow the spatial and temporal structure of radiologist attention and integrates observations in a clinically meaningful sequence. Using a curated dataset of over 30,000 gaze key frames from five radiologists, we demonstrate that GazeX produces more accurate, interpretable, and expert consistent outputs across radiology report generation, disease grounding, and visual question answering, utilizing 231,835 radiographic studies, 780,014 question answer pairs, and 1,162 image sentence pairs with bounding boxes. Unlike autonomous reporting systems, GazeX produces verifiable evidence artifacts, including inspection trajectories and finding linked localized regions, enabling efficient human verification and safe human AI collaboration. Learning through expert eyes provides a practical route toward more trustworthy, explainable, and diagnostically robust AI systems for radiology and beyond.

</details>


### [76] [Credo: Declarative Control of LLM Pipelines via Beliefs and Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14401)
*Duo Lu,Andrew Crotty,Uğur Çetintemel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Credo框架通过信念和声明式策略实现可适应、可审计的智能体系统，解决了传统基于命令式控制循环的AI系统不透明、脆弱的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体系统在需要长期状态决策和持续演化的场景中，依赖命令式控制循环、临时内存和提示嵌入逻辑，导致系统行为不透明、脆弱且难以验证。

Method: Credo将语义状态表示为信念，并使用基于这些信念的声明式策略来规范行为，通过数据库支持的语义控制平面实现可适应、可审计和可组合的执行。

Result: 在决策控制场景中，信念和策略能够声明式地指导关键执行选择（如模型选择、检索、纠正性重新执行），实现动态行为而无需修改底层管道代码。

Conclusion: Credo通过信念和声明式策略的设计，为长期状态决策的AI系统提供了更透明、可适应和可验证的框架，解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are becoming commonplace in domains that require long-lived, stateful decision-making in continuously evolving conditions. As such, correctness depends not only on the output of individual model calls, but also on how to best adapt when incorporating new evidence or revising prior conclusions. However, existing frameworks rely on imperative control loops, ephemeral memory, and prompt-embedded logic, making agent behavior opaque, brittle, and difficult to verify. This paper introduces Credo, which represents semantic state as beliefs and regulates behavior using declarative policies defined over these beliefs. This design supports adaptive, auditable, and composable execution through a database-backed semantic control plane. We showcase these concepts in a decision-control scenario, where beliefs and policies declaratively guide critical execution choices (e.g., model selection, retrieval, corrective re-execution), enabling dynamic behavior without requiring any changes to the underlying pipeline code.

</details>


### [77] [Equifinality in Mixture of Experts: Routing Topology Does Not Determine Language Modeling Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14419)
*Ivan Ternovtsii,Yurii Bilak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现路由拓扑结构并不决定语言模型的渐近困惑度，五种余弦路由变体在统计上等效，这一发现扩展到哈希、随机固定和top-1路由


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究稀疏混合专家架构中路由拓扑是否真正决定语言建模质量，挑战当前越来越复杂的路由机制设计

Method: 构建几何MoE（ST-MoE），使用余弦相似度路由在低维空间（d_space=64）中针对学习到的质心进行路由，比标准线性路由器减少80%路由参数。通过62个对照实验在WikiText-103上进行，参数规模76-84M，训练至收敛（50K步，1.64B tokens）

Result: 路由拓扑不决定渐近困惑度：五种余弦路由变体在1-PPL范围内统计等效（TOST检验p<0.05）。这一发现扩展到哈希、随机固定和top-1路由（单种子实验显示1.1-2.2 PPL优雅退化）。标准线性路由器（5.3倍更多路由参数）达到PPL 32.76，但等参数余弦路由缩小了67%的差距。机制解释为收敛冗余：多跳更新是共线的（cos(Δh_0, Δh_1)=0.805），实现幅度放大而非组合推理

Conclusion: 路由拓扑结构在语言建模质量中作用有限，复杂路由机制的优势很小（约1.2%）。实际应用上，零射相对范数停止可节省25% MoE FLOPs且仅增加0.12% PPL。专家级专业化和因果可控性与拓扑级等效性共存

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures employ increasingly sophisticated routing mechanisms -- learned routers, multi-hop trajectories, token-dependent gating.
